Title: Origenis Opera Omnia, Tomus 5: in Evangelium Matthaei commentariorum, Pars III, Homiliae in Evangelium Lucae et Fragmenta
Creator: Origen; edited by Carolus and Carol. Vincent. De la Rue (Maurists), newly revised by Carol. Henric. Eduard. Lommatzsch
Publisher: Berolini (Berlin): Sumtibus Haude et Spener, 1835
Publication Date: 1835
Rights: Public Domain
Language: grc (Origen's Greek text); lat (Latin translation and series where the Greek is lost, and Lommatzsch's notes)
Rights basis: The item's metadata carries Public Domain Mark 1.0 (licenseurl creativecommons.org/publicdomain/mark/1.0/), and the imprint year is 1835, so it is out of US copyright by date as well.
Source: Internet Archive item origenous-ta-heuriskomena-panta-t.-5-1835, fetched from the item's own OCR text file on 2026-10-26 by the Library thread.
Provenance note: Host OCR text, unchanged apart from this header. The title page was read in the OCR, and the text was read at three points.
Content note: in Evangelium Matthaei commentariorum, Pars III, Homiliae in Evangelium Lucae et Fragmenta. The OCR reads as Greek and Latin with occasional misreadings of accents and breathings and stray marks from the footnote apparatus. Check any quotation against the page image before it goes into a record. Volume 1 of this set (the first part of the Commentary on John) was not vendored: its host OCR is unusable noise.

satis praestet. Suis!) tamen laborat quoque vitiis,

tione in fine operis sui denuntiaverit, se ,,in hoc

nobis loquatur angelus Domini, Si vero mala fuerint in 
nostro corde versata, loquitur nobis angelus diaboli.

Sed in Matthaeo semper generationis nomen adjungitur: 
hic vero penitus siletur. Scriptum est enim ibi: ,AÀbra- 
ham?) genuit Isaac: Isaac genuit Jacob: Jacob genuit

'Thamar:* et usque ad finem »genuit^ semper apponitur.

thaeo non est scriptum: ,incipiebat:* hic vero, quia de 
baptismate conscensurus erat, incipiebat legitur, Scri- 
ptura referente: jet *) ipse erat Jesus incipiens." Quasdé, 
enim. baptizatus est, et mysterium secundae generationis 
assumsit, ut tu quoque priorem nativitatém destruas, et J^ 
in secunda regeneratione nascaris, tunc dicitur incepisse.

nem habet?* Qui respondentes dixerunt: , Caesaris. 5)
